\doxysubsection*{\+: name of the device function}
\label{_amgrp725e2d7c83e8e67981cb01d43e2d2d87}%
struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsubsys__interface}{subsys\+\_\+interface}} -\/ interfaces to device functions

@subsys\+: subsystem of the devices to attach to @node\+: the list of functions registered at the subsystem @add\+\_\+dev\+: device hookup to device function handler @remove\+\_\+dev\+: device hookup to device function handler

Simple interfaces attached to a subsystem. Multiple interfaces can attach to a subsystem and its devices. Unlike drivers, they do not exclusively claim or control devices. Interfaces usually represent a specific functionality of a subsystem/class of devices. \begin{DoxyCompactItemize}

devm\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+percpu -\/ Resource-\/managed alloc\+\_\+percpu @dev\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} to allocate per-\/cpu memory for @type\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolType-p}{Type}} to allocate per-\/cpu memory for

enum device\+\_\+link\+\_\+state -\/ \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} link states. @\+D\+L\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+N\+O\+NE\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} presence of the drivers is not being tracked. @\+D\+L\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+D\+O\+R\+M\+A\+NT\+: None of the supplier/consumer drivers is present. @\+D\+L\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+A\+V\+A\+I\+L\+A\+B\+LE\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} supplier driver is present, but the consumer is not. @\+D\+L\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+C\+O\+N\+S\+U\+M\+E\+R\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+BE\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} consumer is probing (supplier driver present). @\+D\+L\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+A\+C\+T\+I\+VE\+: Both the supplier and consumer drivers are present. @\+D\+L\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+S\+U\+P\+P\+L\+I\+E\+R\+\_\+\+U\+N\+B\+I\+ND\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} supplier driver is unbinding. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{device\_removable}{device\_removable}}

enum device\+\_\+removable -\/ Whether the device is removable. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} criteria for a device to be classified as removable is determined by its subsystem or bus. @\+D\+E\+V\+I\+C\+E\+\_\+\+R\+E\+M\+O\+V\+A\+B\+L\+E\+\_\+\+N\+O\+T\+\_\+\+S\+U\+P\+P\+O\+R\+T\+ED\+: This attribute is not supported for this device (default). @\+D\+E\+V\+I\+C\+E\+\_\+\+R\+E\+M\+O\+V\+A\+B\+L\+E\+\_\+\+U\+N\+K\+N\+O\+WN\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} location is Unknown. @\+D\+E\+V\+I\+C\+E\+\_\+\+F\+I\+X\+ED\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} is not removable by the user. @\+D\+E\+V\+I\+C\+E\+\_\+\+R\+E\+M\+O\+V\+A\+B\+LE\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} is removable by the user. \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}

dev\+\_\+bus\+\_\+name -\/ Return a device\textquotesingle{}s bus/class name, if at all possible @dev\+: struct device to get the bus/class name of

Will return the name of the bus/class the device is attached to. If it is not attached to a bus/class, an empty string will be returned. \mbox{\Hypertarget{include_2linux_2device_8h_a4d37b1cc8c2d5cbfc89aab433708fb3c}\label{include_2linux_2device_8h_a4d37b1cc8c2d5cbfc89aab433708fb3c}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{devm\_ioremap\_resource()}{devm\_ioremap\_resource()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void \mbox{\hyperlink{compiler__types_8h_af6b4077d1d8ce49175e6fcb91788634a}{\+\_\+\+\_\+iomem}}$\ast$ devm\+\_\+ioremap\+\_\+resource (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structdevice}{device}} $\ast$}]{dev,  }\item[{const struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structresource}{resource}} $\ast$}]{res }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

\mbox{\hyperlink{lib_2devres_8c_a2f26d33000ddf1cd63e0e738eb9764bf}{devm\+\_\+ioremap\+\_\+resource()}} -\/ check, request region, and ioremap resource @dev\+: generic device to handle the resource for @res\+: resource to be handled

Checks that a resource is a valid memory region, requests the memory region and ioremaps it. All operations are managed and will be undone on driver detach.

Usage example\+:

res = platform\+\_\+get\+\_\+resource(pdev, I\+O\+R\+E\+S\+O\+U\+R\+C\+E\+\_\+\+M\+E\+M, 0); base = devm\+\_\+ioremap\+\_\+resource(\&pdev-\/$>$dev, res); if (I\+S\+\_\+\+E\+R\+R(base)) return P\+T\+R\+\_\+\+E\+R\+R(base);

Return\+: a pointer to the remapped memory or an \mbox{\hyperlink{err_8h_ae49164ef6cf8117ec9f2fa9a8bb280ea}{E\+R\+R\+\_\+\+P\+T\+R()}} encoded error code on failure. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
